cholesky/cholesky.cpp
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
cholesky/CMakeLists.txt
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cholesky_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(cholesky cholesky.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cholesky PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(cholesky m)

cholesky/build/cmake_install.cmake
# Install script for directory: /home/petert/llm_eval/benchmarks/cholesky

# Set the install prefix
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX)
  set(CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX "/usr/local")
endif()
string(REGEX REPLACE "/$" "" CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}")

# Set the install configuration name.
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME)
  if(BUILD_TYPE)
    string(REGEX REPLACE "^[^A-Za-z0-9_]+" ""
           CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "${BUILD_TYPE}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "")
  endif()
  message(STATUS "Install configuration: \"${CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME}\"")
endif()

# Set the component getting installed.
if(NOT CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  if(COMPONENT)
    message(STATUS "Install component: \"${COMPONENT}\"")
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT "${COMPONENT}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  endif()
endif()

# Install shared libraries without execute permission?
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_INSTALL_SO_NO_EXE)
  set(CMAKE_INSTALL_SO_NO_EXE "1")
endif()

# Is this installation the result of a crosscompile?
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CROSSCOMPILING)
  set(CMAKE_CROSSCOMPILING "FALSE")
endif()

# Set path to fallback-tool for dependency-resolution.
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_OBJDUMP)
  set(CMAKE_OBJDUMP "/usr/bin/objdump")
endif()

if(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  if(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT MATCHES "^[a-zA-Z0-9_.+-]+$")
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_MANIFEST "install_manifest_${CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT}.txt")
  else()
    string(MD5 CMAKE_INST_COMP_HASH "${CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT}")
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_MANIFEST "install_manifest_${CMAKE_INST_COMP_HASH}.txt")

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t n = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix
    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
    
    if (validate) {
        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
    }
    
    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    if (!success) {
        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+            }

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();

+    #pragma omp parallel

+            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < tileCount;
+                 ++diagonalBlock) {
+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =
+                    std::min(kTileSize, n - diagonal);
+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                // A diagonal tile can be factored after all preceding trailing
+                // updates to that tile have completed.
+                #pragma omp task \

+                    firstprivate(diagonal, diagonalSize, diagonalToken)
+                {

+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,
+                                               std::memory_order_release);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;

+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+

+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(matrix + row * n + diagonal, n,

+

+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t leftToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+

+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,

+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {

+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)

+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {

+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,

+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,

+                                }

+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const size_t stride,
+                    const size_t rows, const size_t tileSize) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,

+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {

+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }

+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const size_t stride,
+                    const size_t rows, const size_t tileSize) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * panel[j * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            panel[row * stride + j] =
+                (panel[row * stride + j] - sum) /
+                panel[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)
+                                               : columns;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < lastColumn; ++column) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}

codex
The first compile exposed a GCC/OpenMP requirement: dependency locators must be based on a raw pointer or array, not a `std::vector` expression. I’m adjusting the dependency-token storage to keep the vector alive while passing its raw data pointer to OpenMP; the task graph itself remains unchanged.
apply patch

-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
+                const double value = tile[i * stride + j] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
                     return false;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                tile[i * stride + j] = std::sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                tile[i * stride + j] =
+                    (tile[i * stride + j] - sum) / tile[j * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const size_t stride,
+                    const size_t rows, const size_t tileSize) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * panel[j * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            panel[row * stride + j] =
+                (panel[row * stride + j] - sum) /
+                panel[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)
+                                               : columns;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < lastColumn; ++column) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyStorage(tileCount * tileCount);
+    int* const dependencyTokens = dependencyStorage.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < tileCount;
+                 ++diagonalBlock) {
+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =
+                    std::min(kTileSize, n - diagonal);
+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                // A diagonal tile can be factored after all preceding trailing
+                // updates to that tile have completed.
+                #pragma omp task \
+                    depend(inout: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                    firstprivate(diagonal, diagonalSize, diagonalToken)
+                {
+                    if (positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,
+                                               std::memory_order_release);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    #pragma omp task \
+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(matrix + row * n + diagonal, n,
+                                           rowSize, diagonalSize);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Update every lower-triangular tile in the trailing matrix.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t leftToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+
+                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        true);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken], \
+                                       dependencyTokens[rightToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        false);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // The original routine leaves the upper triangle cleared.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < n; ++row) {
+            for (size_t column = row + 1; column < n; ++column) {
+                matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
+
+    if (!positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               failedDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
+        return false;
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
+                const double value = tile[i * stride + j] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
                     return false;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                tile[i * stride + j] = std::sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
+                tile[i * stride + j] =
+                    (tile[i * stride + j] - sum) / tile[j * stride + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const size_t stride,
+                    const size_t rows, const size_t tileSize) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * panel[j * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            panel[row * stride + j] =
+                (panel[row * stride + j] - sum) /
+                panel[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)
+                                               : columns;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < lastColumn; ++column) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyStorage(tileCount * tileCount);
+    int* const dependencyTokens = dependencyStorage.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < tileCount;
+                 ++diagonalBlock) {
+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =
+                    std::min(kTileSize, n - diagonal);
+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                // A diagonal tile can be factored after all preceding trailing
+                // updates to that tile have completed.
+                #pragma omp task \
+                    depend(inout: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                    firstprivate(diagonal, diagonalSize, diagonalToken)
+                {
+                    if (positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,
+                                               std::memory_order_release);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    #pragma omp task \
+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(matrix + row * n + diagonal, n,
+                                           rowSize, diagonalSize);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Update every lower-triangular tile in the trailing matrix.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t leftToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+
+                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        true);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken], \
+                                       dependencyTokens[rightToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        false);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
+
+            #pragma omp taskwait
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+
+        // The original routine leaves the upper triangle cleared.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t row = 0; row < n; ++row) {
+            for (size_t column = row + 1; column < n; ++column) {
+                matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
+
+    if (!positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n",
+               failedDiagonal.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
+        return false;
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const size_t stride,
+                    const size_t rows, const size_t tileSize) {
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * panel[j * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            panel[row * stride + j] =
+                (panel[row * stride + j] - sum) /
+                panel[j * stride + j];
+        }
+    }

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)
+                                               : columns;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < lastColumn; ++column) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyStorage(tileCount * tileCount);
+    int* const dependencyTokens = dependencyStorage.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < tileCount;
+                 ++diagonalBlock) {
+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =
+                    std::min(kTileSize, n - diagonal);
+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                // A diagonal tile can be factored after all preceding trailing
+                // updates to that tile have completed.
+                #pragma omp task \
+                    depend(inout: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                    firstprivate(diagonal, diagonalSize, diagonalToken)
+                {
+                    if (positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,
+                                               std::memory_order_release);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    #pragma omp task \
+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(matrix + row * n + diagonal, n,
+                                           rowSize, diagonalSize);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Update every lower-triangular tile in the trailing matrix.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t leftToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+
+                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        true);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken], \
+                                       dependencyTokens[rightToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        false);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }

             }

+            #pragma omp taskwait

+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const double* const diagonal,
+                    const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                    const size_t tileSize) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)
+                                               : columns;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < lastColumn; ++column) {

+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyStorage(tileCount * tileCount);
+    int* const dependencyTokens = dependencyStorage.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel
+    {
+        #pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t diagonalBlock = 0; diagonalBlock < tileCount;
+                 ++diagonalBlock) {
+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =
+                    std::min(kTileSize, n - diagonal);
+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                // A diagonal tile can be factored after all preceding trailing
+                // updates to that tile have completed.
+                #pragma omp task \
+                    depend(inout: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                    firstprivate(diagonal, diagonalSize, diagonalToken)
+                {
+                    if (positiveDefinite.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,
+                                               std::memory_order_release);
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    #pragma omp task \
+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(
+                                matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal, n, rowSize,
+                                diagonalSize);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Update every lower-triangular tile in the trailing matrix.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t leftToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+
+                        if (rowBlock == columnBlock) {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(
+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                        matrix + column * n + diagonal, n,
+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,
+                                        true);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+                            #pragma omp task \
+                                depend(in: dependencyTokens[leftToken], \
+                                       dependencyTokens[rightToken]) \
+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
+                            {
+                                if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                        std::memory_order_acquire)) {

+                                        matrix + row * n + column,
+                                        matrix + row * n + diagonal,

+                                        rowSize, columnSize, diagonalSize,

+                }

+// Solve panel * diagonal^T = panel in place, one independent row at a time.
+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const double* const diagonal,
+                    const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                    const size_t tileSize) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < tileSize; ++j) {
+            double sum = 0.0;
+
+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
+                sum += panel[row * stride + k] * diagonal[j * stride + k];
+            }

+                diagonal[j * stride + j];

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,
+                        const double* const right, const size_t stride,
+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(row + 1, columns)

+            #pragma omp simd reduction(+:sum)
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < inner; ++k) {
+                sum += left[row * stride + k] * right[column * stride + k];
+            }
+
+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular

bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // A is stored in row-major order
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            
            if (i == j) {
                // Diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
                if (val <= 0.0) {
                    // Matrix is not positive definite
                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
                    return false;
                }
                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
            } else {
                // Off-diagonal element
                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
                }
                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
            }
        }
        
        // Zero out upper triangular part
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
    
    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    
    // Generate random matrix B
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
    }
    
    // Compute A = B * B^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
            }
            A[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        A[i * n + i] += n;
    }
}

bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
    
    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
    
    // Compute L * L^T
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];
            }
            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;
        }
    }
    
    // Compare with original
    double maxError = 0.0;
    double relError = 0.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);
        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);
        
        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);
        relError = std::max(relError, rel);
    }
    
    printf("Max absolute error: %.10e\n", maxError);
    printf("Max relative error: %.10e\n", relError);
    
    // Check if error is within tolerance
    if (relError > 1e-6) {
        printf("Validation failed: relative error too large\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

    // Allocate matrix
    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
    std::vector<double> A_orig;
    
    // Generate positive definite matrix

+void solvePanelTile(double* const panel, const double* const diagonal,
+                    const size_t stride, const size_t rows,
+                    const size_t tileSize) {
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {

+// Apply C -= A * B^T to one lower-triangular output tile.
+void updateTrailingTile(double* const output, const double* const left,

+                        const size_t rows, const size_t columns,
+                        const size_t inner, const bool diagonalTile) {

+            output[row * stride + column] -= sum;

+// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.
+// The tiled task graph exposes the dependencies between diagonal factorization,
+// panel solves, and trailing updates to OpenMP's scheduler.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+    std::vector<int> dependencyStorage(tileCount * tileCount);
+    int* const dependencyTokens = dependencyStorage.data();
+    std::atomic<bool> positiveDefinite{true};
+    std::atomic<size_t> failedDiagonal{n};
+    double* const matrix = A.data();

+                const size_t diagonal = diagonalBlock * kTileSize;
+                const size_t diagonalSize =

+                const size_t diagonalToken =
+                    diagonalBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+

+                // updates to that tile have completed.

+                        !factorDiagonalTile(matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal,
+                                            n, diagonalSize)) {
+                        size_t expected = n;
+                        failedDiagonal.compare_exchange_strong(
+                            expected, diagonal, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                        positiveDefinite.store(false,

-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+
+                // Solve all panel tiles below the diagonal tile.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {
+                    const size_t row = rowBlock * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);
+                    const size_t panelToken =
+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+

+                        depend(in: dependencyTokens[diagonalToken]) \
+                        depend(inout: dependencyTokens[panelToken]) \
+                        firstprivate(row, rowSize, diagonalSize, panelToken)
+                    {
+                        if (positiveDefinite.load(
+                                std::memory_order_acquire)) {
+                            solvePanelTile(
+                                matrix + row * n + diagonal,
+                                matrix + diagonal * n + diagonal, n, rowSize,
+                                diagonalSize);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Update every lower-triangular tile in the trailing matrix.
+                for (size_t rowBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                     rowBlock < tileCount; ++rowBlock) {

+                    const size_t rowSize = std::min(kTileSize, n - row);

+                        rowBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+
+                    for (size_t columnBlock = diagonalBlock + 1;
+                         columnBlock <= rowBlock; ++columnBlock) {
+                        const size_t column = columnBlock * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t columnSize =
+                            std::min(kTileSize, n - column);
+                        const size_t rightToken =
+                            columnBlock * tileCount + diagonalBlock;
+                        const size_t outputToken =
+                            rowBlock * tileCount + columnBlock;
+

+                                depend(inout: dependencyTokens[outputToken]) \
+                                firstprivate(row, rowSize, column, columnSize, \
+                                             diagonal, diagonalSize, outputToken)
